     6: const formatSyncTime = (value) => {
     7:   if (!value) return "조회 전";
     8:   const parsed = new Date(value);
     9:   if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) return String(value);
    10:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ko-KR", { month:"2-digit", day:"2-digit", hour:"2-digit", minute:"2-digit", hour12:false }).format(parsed);
    11: };
    12: const addDateDays = (dateText, days) => {
    13:   const date = new Date(`${dateText || dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
    14:   if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "";
    15:   date.setDate(date.getDate() + Number(days || 0));
    16:   return dateKey(date);
    17: };
    18: const daysFromToday = (dateText) => {
    19:   const due = new Date(`${dateText}T12:00:00`), now = new Date(`${dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
    20:   return Number.isNaN(due.getTime()) ? null : Math.round((due-now)/86400000);
    21: };

  1499: function nightStudyOperationStatus(key){
  1500:   const date=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
  1501:   const rules=readNightStudyRules().filter(r=>r&&r.active!==false);
  1502:   for(const r of rules){
  1503:     if(r.type==='range'&&r.start&&r.end&&key>=r.start&&key<=r.end)return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'미운영 기간'};
  1504:     if(r.type==='weekday'&&Number(r.weekday)===date.getDay())return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'요일 미운영'};
  1505:   }
  1506:   return {operates:true,reason:'운영일'};
  1507: }

  1546: function dormRuleCategoryLabel(category){
  1547:   return ({period:'학교 운영기간',closure:'학사 미운영기간',exception:'입·퇴소 변경',exam:'시험 외출허용',vacation:'방학 중 외출허용',base:'기본 입·퇴소'})[category]||'기타 규칙';

  1549: function dormRuleNormalizeDateTime(value){
  1550:   if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='') return '';
  1551:   // Google Sheets 날짜 직렬값은 시트의 '현지 날짜/시간' 값이다.
  1552:   // UTC instant로 해석한 뒤 Asia/Seoul을 다시 적용하면 +9시간이 생기므로,
  1553:   // 직렬값의 날짜/시간 성분을 그대로 추출한다.
  1554:   if(typeof value==='number'||/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(String(value).trim())){
  1555:     const serial=Number(value);
  1556:     if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000){
  1557:       const d=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+Math.round(serial*86400000));
  1558:       if(!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())){
  1559:         const y=d.getUTCFullYear(),m=String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),day=String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0');
  1560:         const hh=String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2,'0'),mm=String(d.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
  1561:         return `${y}-${m}-${day}T${hh}:${mm}`;
  1562:       }
  1563:     }
  1564:   }
  1565:   const raw=String(value).trim().replace(' ','T');
  1566:   const m=raw.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})T(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  1568:   return String(value).trim();
  1569: }
  1570: function dormRuleDateText(value){
  1571:   const v=dormRuleNormalizeDateTime(value);
  1572:   return v?`${v.slice(0,10)}${v.length>10?` ${v.slice(11,16)}`:''}`:'-';
  1573: }
  1574: function dormRuleAppliesAt(rule,dateLike){
  1575:   if(!rule?.active)return false;
  1576:   const stamp=(dateLike instanceof Date?dateLike:new Date(dateLike)).getTime();
  1577:   const a=new Date(rule.start||'1900-01-01T00:00').getTime(), b=new Date(rule.end||'2999-12-31T23:59').getTime();
  1578:   return Number.isFinite(stamp)&&stamp>=a&&stamp<=b;
  1579: }
  1580: function dormOutingIsMedical(item){
  1581:   const text=[item?.category,item?.reason,item?.destination,item?.type,item?.outingType].map(v=>String(v||'').trim()).join(' ');
  1582:   return /병원|의원|진료|치과|한의원|의료/.test(text);
  1583: }
  1584: function dormOutingExcludedByRule(item){
  1585:   // 병원 외출은 학사운영규칙의 학원외출 허용/미운영 판정과 무관한 개인 외출사유입니다.
  1586:   // 신청이 존재하면 오늘의 학사외출현황과 메일/출력 대상에서 제외하지 않습니다.
  1587:   if(dormOutingIsMedical(item))return false;
  1588:   const movementKind=[item?.category,item?.type,item?.outingType,item?.status,item?.note].map(v=>String(v||'')).join(' ');
  1589:   if(/늦은\s*입소|재입소|입소|외출\s*후\s*복귀|복귀/.test(movementKind))return false;
  1590:   if(item?.operationIncluded===false)return true;
  1591:   const value=String(item?.date||'').slice(0,10);
  1592:   if(!value)return false;
  1593:   const stamp=new Date(`${value}T12:00:00`);
  1594:   return dormRuleHistory().some(rule=>rule.category==='closure'&&dormRuleAppliesAt(rule,stamp));
  1595: }

  1599: function dormRuleEffectDefault(category){
  1600:   return ({period:'운영기간',closure:'학사 미운영(퇴소~입소 자동기간)',base:'기본 주간운영',exception:'입퇴소 변경(외출허용 기본상태 유지)',exam:'학원외출 특별허용',vacation:'방학 중 학원외출 특별허용'})[category]||'';
  1601: }
  1602: function dormRuleWeekdaysFromRange(start,end){
  1603:   const a=new Date(start),b=new Date(end);if(Number.isNaN(a.getTime())||Number.isNaN(b.getTime()))return '전체';
  1604:   const names=['일','월','화','수','목','금','토'],seen=[];
  1605:   const d=new Date(a);d.setHours(12,0,0,0);const stop=new Date(b);stop.setHours(12,0,0,0);
  1606:   let guard=0;while(d<=stop&&guard++<8){const n=names[d.getDay()];if(!seen.includes(n))seen.push(n);d.setDate(d.getDate()+1);}
  1607:   return guard>=8?'전체':seen.join(',');
  1608: }

  1695: async function deleteDormRule(ruleId){
  1696:   const rules=dormRuleHistory(), rule=rules.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(ruleId));if(!rule)return;
  1697:   if(!confirm(`${rule.name} 규칙을 규칙로그 시트에서도 삭제할까요?`))return;

  1757: function dormTermDefinition(mode){
  1758:   return ({
  1759:     g1s1:{label:'1학년 1학기',grade:1,semester:'1',year:'2026'},
  1760:     g1s2:{label:'1학년 2학기',grade:1,semester:'2',year:'2026'},
  1761:     g2s1:{label:'2학년 1학기',grade:2,semester:'1',year:'2027'},
  1762:     g2s2:{label:'2학년 2학기',grade:2,semester:'2',year:'2027'},
  1763:     g3:{label:'3학년',grade:3,semester:'',year:'2028'}
  1764:   })[mode]||null;
  1765: }
  1766: function dormStudentNoSort(a,b){
  1767:   return String(a.studentNo||'').localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||''),'ko',{numeric:true})||String(a.name||'').localeCompare(String(b.name||''),'ko');
  1768: }
  1769: function dormTermRoster(mode){
  1770:   const term=dormTermDefinition(mode);if(!term)return [];
  1771:   const all=[...(readonlyCache?.dormHistory||[]),...(readonlyCache?.pastDormStudents||[]),...(readonlyCache?.dormStudents||[])];
  1772:   const seen=new Set();
  1773:   return all.filter(row=>{
  1774:     const year=String(row.year||'').replace(/\.0$/,'');
  1775:     const sem=String(row.semester||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');
  1776:     const no=String(row.studentNo||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
  1777:     const inferredGrade=no?Number(no[0]):Number(String(row.className||'').match(/^([123])[- ]/)?.[1]||1);
  1778:     const grade=Number(row.grade||inferredGrade||1);
  1779:     if(year&&term.year&&year!==term.year)return false;
  1780:     if(grade!==term.grade)return false;
  1781:     if(term.semester&&sem&&sem!==term.semester)return false;
  1782:     if(term.semester&&!sem){
  1783:       const start=String(row.startDate||'');
  1784:       if(start){const month=Number(start.slice(5,7));if(term.semester==='1'&&month>=8)return false;if(term.semester==='2'&&month>0&&month<7)return false;}
  1785:     }
  1786:     const key=`${row.studentId||row.studentNo}|${term.label}`;if(seen.has(key))return false;seen.add(key);return true;
  1787:   }).sort(dormStudentNoSort);
  1788: }
  1789: function dormScoreRows(studentId){return (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(row=>String(row.studentId||'')===String(studentId||''));}
  1790: function dormAverage(rows){const values=rows.map(row=>Number(row.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);return values.length?values.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/values.length:null;}
  1847:   const result=[];
  1848:   years.forEach(year=>order.forEach(exam=>{const avg=dormAverage(rows.filter(row=>String(row.schoolYear||'')===year&&row.exam===exam));if(avg!=null)result.push({label:`${year.slice(-2)}.${exam.replace('모의고사','').replace('월','')}`,value:avg});}));
  1849:   return result;
  1850: }

  2077: function attendanceStudentKey(row) {
  2078:   // 출결 원본은 학생ID가 비어 있거나 서로 다른 형식일 수 있으므로 학번을 최우선 기준으로 사용합니다.
  2079:   return String(row?.studentNo || row?.studentId || `${row?.name || ""}|${row?.className || ""}`);
  2127:   const text = String(label || "").trim();
  2128:   if (!text || text === "-" || !/(?:^|\D)[123]-?\d(?:\D|$)|\d반/.test(text)) return { cls:"", style:"" };
  2129:   const palette = [
  2130:     ["#e9f6ff","#63a9d8","#174f72"], ["#eef7e8","#78ad61","#315d28"],
  2131:     ["#fff2e8","#dd9362","#7a4523"], ["#f3ecff","#9b79d0","#50367a"],
  2132:     ["#e8f8f4","#57ab98","#245e52"], ["#fff0f4","#d9829b","#77384b"],
  2133:     ["#f7f3df","#b8a75b","#665a21"], ["#eaf0ff","#718ed2","#354c86"],
  2134:     ["#f0f4f7","#879caf","#425568"]
  2135:   ];
  2136:   let hash = 0; for (const ch of text) hash = (hash * 31 + ch.charCodeAt(0)) >>> 0;
  2137:   const [fill,border,color] = palette[hash % palette.length];
  2138:   return { cls:"class-colored", style:` style="--class-fill:${fill};--class-border:${border};--class-text:${color}"` };
  2139: }
  2140: function timetableSubjectStyle(subject) {
  2141:   const text=String(subject||"").replace(/\s+/g,"");
  2142:   const rules=[
  2143:     [/국어|한문/,"subject-korean"],[/수학/,"subject-math"],[/영어/,"subject-english"],
  2144:     [/사회|한국사|역사|지리|윤리/,"subject-social"],[/과학|물리|화학|생명|지구/,"subject-science"],
  2145:     [/진로|동아리|자율|창체/,"subject-creative"],[/체육|음악|미술/,"subject-art"]
  2146:   ];
  2147:   return rules.find(([pattern])=>pattern.test(text))?.[1]||"subject-other";
  2148: }

  2150: function shiftDateKey(key, delta) {
  2151:   const date = new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
  2152:   if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return dateKey(today);
  2153:   date.setDate(date.getDate() + delta);
  2154:   return dateKey(date);
  2155: }

  2159: function nightSlotName(rowOrText) {
  2160:   const text = typeof rowOrText === "string" ? rowOrText : `${rowOrText?.time || ""} ${rowOrText?.type || ""} ${rowOrText?.detail || ""}`;
  2161:   // 운호고 방과후 8교시는 오후자습 시간대이다.
  2162:   if (/오후자습|1타임|8교시/.test(text)) return "오후자습";
  2163:   if (/야자\s*1|야간\s*1|2타임/.test(text)) return "야자1";
  2164:   if (/야자\s*2|야간\s*2|3타임/.test(text)) return "야자2";
  2165:   return String(rowOrText?.time || rowOrText?.type || text || "기타").trim();
  2166: }
  2167: function minutesOfClock(value) {
  2168:   const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  2169:   return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null;
  2170: }

  2903: function inputCenterHumanReportText(value=''){
  2904:   const source=String(value||'').trim();if(!source)return '';
  2905:   try{
  2906:     const meta=JSON.parse(source);
  2907:     const fields=Array.isArray(meta?.fields)?meta.fields:Array.isArray(meta)?meta:[];
  2908:     const skip=/^(연락처|전화번호|휴대폰|서명|이메일|학번|성명|이름|학년|반|수정시간|제출시간|제출일시)$/;
  2909:     const pairs=fields.map((f,i)=>({label:String(f?.label||`문항 ${i+1}`).trim(),value:String(f?.value||'').trim()})).filter(x=>x.value&&!skip.test(x.label));
  2910:     if(pairs.length)return pairs.map(x=>`[${x.label||'문항'}]\n${x.value}`).join('\n\n');
  2911:   }catch{}
  2912:   return source;
  2913: }
  2914: function inputCenterReviewDisplayText(row={}){
  2915:   const reviewed=inputCenterHumanReportText(row.reviewText||'');
  2916:   if(reviewed)return reviewed;
  2917:   const structured=inputCenterHumanReportText(row.normalizedText||'');
  2918:   if(structured&&!/^\s*[\[{]/.test(structured))return structured;
  2919:   if(structured&&structured!==String(row.normalizedText||'').trim())return structured;
  2920:   return String(row.rawText||row.text||'').trim();
  2921: }

  4119: function gradeBadgeMarkup(value, suffix = "등급") {
  4120:   if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">-</span>`;
  4121:   const number = Number(value);
  4122:   if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">${escapeHtml(String(value))}</span>`;
  4123:   return `<span class="grade-badge ${gradeToneClass(number)}"><b>${scoreDisplay(number,2)}</b><small>${suffix}</small></span>`;
  4124: }
  4125: function mockGradeMiniMarkup(studentId, exam) {
  4126:   const order = ["국어", "수학", "영어", "한국사"];
  4127:   const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => row.studentId === studentId && row.scoreType === "모의고사" && row.exam === exam);
  4128:   if (!rows.length) return `<span class="mock-grade-empty">자료 없음</span>`;
  4129:   const sorted = rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
  4130:     const ai=order.indexOf(a.subject), bi=order.indexOf(b.subject);
  4131:     if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi);
  4132:     return String(a.subject).localeCompare(String(b.subject),"ko");
  4133:   });
  4134:   return `<div class="mock-grade-mini-strip">${sorted.map(row=>`<span><small>${escapeHtml(row.subject)}</small><b class="${gradeToneClass(row.level)}">${escapeHtml(String(row.level || "-"))}</b></span>`).join("")}</div>`;

  4136: function scoreTableMarkup(rows, mode = scoreMode) {
  4137:   const coreOrder = mode === "mock" ? ["국어","수학","영어","한국사"] : ["공통국어1","공통수학1","공통영어1","한국사1","통합사회1","통합과학1","한문","과학탐구실험1","체육1","음악"];
  4138:   const subjects = [...new Set(rows.map(row=>row.subject).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>{
  4139:     const ai=coreOrder.indexOf(a), bi=coreOrder.indexOf(b);
  4140:     if(ai>=0||bi>=0) return (ai<0?999:ai)-(bi<0?999:bi);
  4141:     return a.localeCompare(b,"ko");
  4142:   });
  4143:   const groups = new Map();
  4144:   rows.forEach(row=>{
  4145:     const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
  4146:     const key=`${row.studentId}|${period}|${row.exam}`;
  4147:     if(!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key,{studentId:row.studentId,studentNo:row.studentNo,name:row.name,period,exam:row.exam,items:new Map()});
  4148:     groups.get(key).items.set(row.subject,row);
  4149:   });
  4150:   const grouped=[...groups.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.studentNo)) || a.period.localeCompare(b.period) || scoreExamOrder(a.exam)-scoreExamOrder(b.exam) || String(a.exam).localeCompare(String(b.exam),"ko"));
  4151:   const cell=(row)=>{
  4152:     if(!row) return `<td class="empty-score-cell">-</td>`;
  4153:     const trend=scoreTrend(row,readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]);
  4154:     const trendMarkup=trend.text!=="-"?`<em class="trend ${trend.cls}">${trend.text}</em>`:"";
  4155:     if(row.scoreType==="모의고사") return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(row.level)}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 표준 ${scoreDisplay(row.standardScore)}${row.percentile!==""?` · 백분위 ${scoreDisplay(row.percentile)}`:""}</small></td>`;
  4156:     const gradeValue = row.level === "산출제외" ? (row.achievement || "산출제외") : row.level;
  4157:     return `<td><div class="score-cell-primary">${gradeBadgeMarkup(gradeValue, Number.isFinite(Number(gradeValue))?"등급":"")}${trendMarkup}</div><small>원점수 ${scoreDisplay(row.rawScore)} · 석차 ${escapeHtml(String(row.rank||"-"))}/${escapeHtml(String(row.enrollment||"-"))}${row.achievement?` · ${escapeHtml(row.achievement)}`:""}</small></td>`;
  4158:   };
  4159:   const title = mode === "mock" ? "모의고사 과목별 등급" : "내신 과목별 등급";
  4160:   return `<div class="data-card score-matrix-card"><div class="card-head"><div><strong>${title}</strong></div><small>전체 누적 또는 선택 학기를 표시합니다.</small></div><div class="score-matrix-scroll"><table class="score-matrix-table"><thead><tr><th class="student-col">학생</th><th class="exam-col">학기·시험</th>${subjects.map(subject=>`<th>${escapeHtml(subject)}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${grouped.map(group=>`<tr><th class="student-col"><b>${escapeHtml(group.studentNo)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(group.name)}</small></th><th class="exam-col"><b>${escapeHtml(scorePeriodLabel(group.period).replace("학년도 ","· "))}</b><small>${escapeHtml(group.exam)}</small></th>${subjects.map(subject=>cell(group.items.get(subject))).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div></div>`;
  4161: }

  4163: function scoreDisplay(value, digits = 1) {
  4164:   if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return "-";
  4165:   const number = Number(value);
  4166:   if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return escapeHtml(String(value));
  4167:   return Number.isInteger(number) ? String(number) : number.toFixed(digits).replace(/\.0+$/, "");
  4168: }
  4169: function scoreAverage(rows) {
  4170:   const values = rows.filter((row) => row.level !== "" && row.level !== null && row.level !== undefined).map((row) => Number(row.level)).filter(Number.isFinite);
  4171:   return values.length ? values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length : null;
  4172: }

  4451: function actualNineGrade(row) {
  4452:   let rank = Number(String(row?.rank ?? "").replace(/[^0-9.]/g,""));
  4453:   let enrollment = Number(String(row?.enrollment ?? "").replace(/[^0-9.]/g,""));
  4454:   let derived=false;
  4455:   if((!Number.isFinite(rank)||rank<=0)&&Number.isFinite(scoreNumeric(row?.rawScore))){
  4456:     const raw=scoreNumeric(row.rawScore);
  4457:     const peers=(readonlyCache?.scoreRecords||[]).filter(peer=>
  4458:       peer?.scoreType==="내신"&&String(peer.exam||"")===String(row?.exam||"")&&String(peer.subject||"")===String(row?.subject||"")&&
  4459:       String(peer.year||"")===String(row?.year||"")&&String(peer.semester||"")===String(row?.semester||"")&&Number.isFinite(scoreNumeric(peer.rawScore))
  4460:     );
  4461:     if(peers.length){
  4462:       rank=peers.filter(peer=>scoreNumeric(peer.rawScore)>raw).length+1;
  4463:       if(!Number.isFinite(enrollment)||enrollment<=0)enrollment=peers.length;
  4464:       derived=true;
  4465:     }
  4466:   }
  4467:   if (!Number.isFinite(rank) || !Number.isFinite(enrollment) || rank <= 0 || enrollment <= 0) return null;
  4468:   const percentile = rank / enrollment * 100;
  4469:   const grade = percentile <= 4 ? 1 : percentile <= 11 ? 2 : percentile <= 23 ? 3 : percentile <= 40 ? 4 : percentile <= 60 ? 5 : percentile <= 77 ? 6 : percentile <= 89 ? 7 : percentile <= 96 ? 8 : 9;
  4470:   return { grade, percentile, rank, enrollment, derived, detail:`${rank}/${enrollment}명 = 상위 ${percentile.toFixed(1)}%${derived?' · 원점수 기준 추정석차':''}` };
  4471: }

  4937:     selectedPrograms,

  4943: function studentCareerSupportBase_(student,bundle){
  4944:   const profile=student?.careerProfile||{};
  4945:   const rows=(bundle?.selected||[]);
  4946:   const first=(...values)=>values.map(v=>String(v??"").trim()).find(Boolean)||"-";
  4947:   const hopeTrack=first(profile.hopeTrack,rows[0]?.track,rows[0]?.hopeTrack,rows[0]?.careerTrack,rows[0]?.trackHope,profile.track,student?.hopeTrack,student?.careerTrack,student?.desiredTrack);
  4948:   const hopeMajor=first(profile.hopeMajor,rows[0]?.major,rows[0]?.hopeMajor,rows[0]?.careerMajor,rows[0]?.majorHope,profile.major,student?.hopeMajor,student?.careerMajor,student?.desiredMajor);
  4949:   const aptitude=first(profile.universityAptitude,student?.universityAptitude,student?.aptitudeData,profile.aptitude,student?.careerAptitude,student?.career,"-");
  4950:   const text=`${hopeTrack} ${hopeMajor}`.replace(/-/g,"");

  4952:   const add=(key,rx,w=1)=>{const m=text.match(rx);if(m)scores[key]+=m.length*w;};
  4953:   add("문과",/인문|국문|영문|언어|문학|사학|역사|철학|사회|정치|외교|행정|법학|경제|경영|회계|무역|교육|심리|언론|미디어|관광|사회복지/g);

  4955:   add("예체능",/미술|디자인|음악|성악|작곡|악기|체육|스포츠|무용|연기|연극|영화|영상|애니|만화|공예|사진|실용음악/g);
  4956:   const ranked=Object.entries(scores).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
  4957:   let recommendation="혼합";
  4958:   if(ranked[0][1]>0 && (ranked[1][1]===0 || ranked[0][1]>=ranked[1][1]*2)) recommendation=ranked[0][0];
  4959:   return {hopeTrack,hopeMajor,aptitude,recommendation};
  4960: }

  4984:     .replace(/[\s·ㆍ.,:;()\[\]{}'"`~!@#$%^&*+=_|\\/<>?–—-]+/g, "")

  5583: function tagMatches(text,rx){
  5584:   const re=new RegExp(rx.source,'gi'), matches=[]; let m;
  5585:   while((m=re.exec(text))!==null){matches.push(m[0]); if(m.index===re.lastIndex)re.lastIndex++;}
  5586:   return [...new Set(matches)].slice(0,8);
  5587: }
  5588: function evidenceSentence(text,words){
  5589:   if(!words.length)return '';
  5590:   const parts=String(text||'').split(/(?<=[.!?。]|다\.)\s+|\n+/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  5591:   return parts.find(x=>words.some(w=>x.includes(w)))||parts[0]||'';
  5592: }
  5593: function analyzeReportTags(report){
  5594:   const text=[report?.programName,report?.reportGroup,...(report?.responses||[])].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  5595:   const sdgs=SDG_CATALOG.map(([no,name,rx])=>{const words=tagMatches(text,rx);return {type:'SDGs',key:String(no),label:`SDG ${no} ${name}`,score:words.length,words,sentence:evidenceSentence(text,words)};}).filter(x=>x.score).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||Number(a.key)-Number(b.key)).slice(0,3);
  5596:   const careers=CAREER_TAG_CATALOG.map(([label,key,rx])=>{const words=tagMatches(text,rx);return {type:'진로계열',key,label,score:words.length,words,sentence:evidenceSentence(text,words)};}).filter(x=>x.score).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,3);
  5597:   const majors=MAJOR_TAG_CATALOG.map(([label,rx])=>{const words=tagMatches(text,rx);return {type:'전공군',key:label,label,score:words.length,words,sentence:evidenceSentence(text,words)};}).filter(x=>x.score).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,3);
  5598:   return {sdgs,careers,majors,text};
  5599: }

  5605: function openReportTagDetail(encoded){

  5859:   if(/느꼈|생각했|알게 되었/.test(text))issues.push("단순 소감 표현을 관찰·탐구·성장 중심으로 바꾸는 것이 좋습니다.");

  5861:   if(text.length>recordAreaLimit(area))issues.push("영역별 글자 수 제한을 초과했습니다.");
  5862:   if(/(?:했다|하였다|했습니다|하였습니다|되었다|이었다)\.?\s*$/.test(text))issues.push("최종 문장이 학생 서술형 ‘~했다’로 끝납니다. 교사 관찰형 종결로 수정해야 합니다.");
  5863:   const current=currentRecordStudent();
  5864:   const similar=savedDraftsForArea(area,current?.id).map(x=>({...x,score:jaccardSimilarity(text,x.text)})).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score)[0];
  5865:   if(similar?.score>=0.62){
  5866:     const other=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>s.id===similar.studentId);
  5867:     issues.push(`저장된 ${other?.name||"다른 학생"} 문장과 유사도 ${Math.round(similar.score*100)}%입니다. 활동·역할·강조점을 더 개별화하세요.`);
  5868:   }
  5869:   return issues;
  5870: }

  5880: function selectionErrorsForStudent(student){

  5883:   return rows.filter(x=>((x.studentId&&x.studentId===student?.id)||(x.studentNo&&String(x.studentNo)===String(student?.studentNo)))&&allowed.has(String(x.type||'').trim()));
  5884: }

  5888: function curriculumErrorText(row){
  5889:   return String(row?.detail||[row?.type,row?.subject,row?.terms,row?.status].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||'선택과목 신청 내용을 다시 확인해 주세요.').trim();
  5890: }
  5891: function curriculumErrorCategory(row){
  5892:   const text=`${row?.type||''} ${row?.detail||''} ${row?.subject||''} ${row?.terms||''} ${row?.status||''}`;
  5893:   if(/문.?이과|교차|혼합|사회.*과학|과학.*사회/.test(text))return '문·이과 탐구 혼합';
  5894:   if(/위계|선이수|후속|Ⅰ.*Ⅱ|1.*2/.test(text))return '과목 위계 오류';
  5895:   if(/중복|동일.?과목|학기간/.test(text))return '학기간 중복 신청';
  5896:   if(/개수|과목.?수|부족|초과/.test(text))return '선택 과목 수 확인 필요';
  5897:   return '기타 확인사항';
  5898: }
  5899: function groupedCurriculumErrors(errors){
  5900:   const order=['문·이과 탐구 혼합','과목 위계 오류','학기간 중복 신청','선택 과목 수 확인 필요','기타 확인사항'];
  5901:   const map=new Map(order.map(x=>[x,[]]));
  5902:   (errors||[]).forEach(row=>map.get(curriculumErrorCategory(row)).push(curriculumErrorText(row)));
  5903:   return order.map(title=>({title,items:[...new Set(map.get(title))]})).filter(group=>group.items.length);
  5904: }
  5905: function buildCurriculumReapplicationMessage(student,bundle){
  5906:   const career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle);
  5907:   const errors=selectionErrorsForStudent(student);
  5908:   const terms=['2학년 1학기','2학년 2학기','3학년 1학기','3학년 2학기'];
  5909:   const termBlocks=terms.map(term=>`▶ ${term}\n${curriculumTermSubjects(bundle,term).join(', ')||'신청 없음'}`).join('\n\n');
  5910:   const groups=groupedCurriculumErrors(errors);
  5911:   const submit=selectionSubmissionInfo(bundle);

  5913:   return `[운호고 선택과목 사전점검 안내]\n\n안녕하세요. 운호고등학교 1학년부입니다.\n\n${student?.name||'학생'} 학생의 선택과목 신청 내역을 점검한 결과, 확인이 필요한 사항이 있어 안내드립니다.\n\n■ 신청 현황\n· 최종 신청일 : ${submit.date}\n· 신청 차수 : ${submit.count}차 신청\n· 8월 3일 안내 후 수정 : ${submit.modifiedAfterNotice?'수정함':'추가 수정 없음'}\n\n■ 진로희망\n· 희망계열 : ${career.hopeTrack||'-'}\n· 희망전공 : ${career.hopeMajor||'-'}\n· 추천계열 : ${career.recommendation||'-'}\n\n■ 현재 신청 내역\n\n${termBlocks}\n\n■ 확인이 필요한 사항\n\n${errorBlock}\n\n개학 후 교육과정부의 공식 수강신청이 예정되어 있으므로, 아래 구글폼에서 방학 중 신청 내용을 확인하시고 수정하여 제출하여 주시기 바랍니다.\n\n▶ 선택과목 수정 구글폼\n${SUBJECT_REAPPLICATION_FORM_URL}\n\n※ 본 내용은 사전 상담 및 오류 점검을 위한 안내이며, 최종 선택과목은 개학 후 교육과정부의 공식 수강신청 결과로 확정됩니다.\n\n감사합니다.\n운호고등학교 1학년부`;
  5914: }

  5986: function recordsView() {
  5987:   const allStudents=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  5988:   const classStudents=allStudents.filter(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===String(recordClassNo)).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.number||0)-Number(b.number||0));
  5989:   if(!recordStudentId||!allStudents.some(s=>s.id===recordStudentId))recordStudentId=(classStudents[0]||allStudents[0])?.id||"";
  5990:   let student=allStudents.find(s=>s.id===recordStudentId)||classStudents[0]||allStudents[0];
  5991:   if(student&&recordStudentClass(student)!==String(recordClassNo))recordClassNo=recordStudentClass(student)||recordClassNo;
  5992:   const visibleClassStudents=allStudents.filter(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===String(recordClassNo)).filter(s=>!(recordMode==='curriculum'&&recordCurriculumErrorOnly)||selectionErrorsForStudent(s).length>0).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.number||0)-Number(b.number||0));
  5993:   if(!visibleClassStudents.some(s=>s.id===student?.id))student=visibleClassStudents[0]||student;
  5994:   const bundle=student?studentRecordBundle(student):{activities:[],selected:[],finalRecords:[],programs:[],reports:[]};
  5995:   const tabs=[["activities","활동내역"],["sdgs","SDGs 연계 성장 프로파일"],["curriculum","교육과정"],["final","기록"]];
  5996:   const classCards=Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1)).map(no=>`<button data-record-class="${no}" class="${String(recordClassNo)===no?"active":""}"><b>${no}반</b><span>${allStudents.filter(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===no).length}명</span></button>`).join("");
  5997:   const options=visibleClassStudents.map(s=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}" ${s.id===student?.id?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(s.name)}</option>`).join("");
  5998:   const personalToolbar=`<div class="record-student-toolbar personal-only"><div class="record-class-cards">${classCards}</div><label>${recordClassNo}반 학생<select id="recordStudentSelect">${options}</select></label><button class="btn primary" data-open-student-dashboard="${escapeHtml(student?.id||"")}">학생 개인 대시보드</button></div>`;

  6137: function programDurationHours(program){
  6138:   const direct=Number(program?.hours||program?.durationHours||program?.totalHours);
  6139:   if(Number.isFinite(direct)&&direct>0)return direct;
  6140:   const sessionCount=Number(program?.sessionCount||program?.sessions||String(program?.sessionNo||"").match(/\d+/)?.[0]);
  6141:   if(Number.isFinite(sessionCount)&&sessionCount>0)return sessionCount;
  6142:   const time=String(program?.time||"");
  6143:   const match=time.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*[~〜-]\s*(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  6144:   if(match){const start=Number(match[1])*60+Number(match[2]);const end=Number(match[3])*60+Number(match[4]);if(end>start)return Math.round((end-start)/6)/10;}
  6145:   return program?.date?1:0;
  6146: }

  9178: function studentAccessContext(student){
  9179:   const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
  9180:   const studentClass=String(student?.className||student?.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
  9181:   const ownClass=String(scope.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
  9182:   const role=currentRoleId();
  9183:   const isAdmin=role==="admin";
  9184:   const isOwnHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&ownClass&&studentClass&&String(Number(ownClass))===String(Number(studentClass));
  9185:   const isOtherHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&!isOwnHomeroom;
  9186:   const isReadOnlyLeadership=role==="vice_principal"||role==="principal";
  9187:   return {role,isAdmin,isOwnHomeroom,isOtherHomeroom,isReadOnlyLeadership,canEditContact:!isReadOnlyLeadership&&(isAdmin||isOwnHomeroom)};
  9188: }

  9301:   const text=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(raw);

  9311: function admissionUnitTokens(value){
  9312:   const raw=String(value||'').normalize('NFKC').toLowerCase();
  9313:   if(!raw.trim())return [];
  9314:   const groups=[];
  9315:   const tests=[
  9316:     ['의예',/의예|의학|의과/],['치의예',/치의예|치의학|치과/],['약학',/약학|약대/],['한의예',/한의예|한의학/],
  9317:     ['간호보건',/간호|보건|의료/],['공학',/공학|공과|공대|전자|전기|기계|컴퓨터|소프트웨어|인공지능|반도체|화공|신소재|건축|토목/],
  9318:     ['자연',/자연|이공|과학|수학|물리|화학|생명|환경/],['인문사회',/인문|사회|상경|경영|경제|행정|정치|국문|영문|외국어|사학|철학|교육/],
  9319:     ['예체능',/예체능|예술|체육|미술|음악/]
  9320:   ];
  9321:   tests.forEach(([key,re])=>{if(re.test(raw))groups.push(key);});
  9322:   return groups;
  9323: }
  9324: function admissionPairCompatibility(cut,rule){
  9325:   const cutTrack=admissionTrackKey(cut?.track||cut?.name), ruleTrack=admissionTrackKey(rule?.name||rule?.type);
  9326:   const cutName=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(cut?.name), ruleName=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(rule?.name);
  9327:   const cutUnit=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(cut?.unit), ruleUnit=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(rule?.unit);
  9328:   const cutGroups=admissionUnitTokens(cut?.unit), ruleGroups=admissionUnitTokens(rule?.unit);
  9329:   const trackExact=Boolean(cutName&&ruleName&&cutName===ruleName);
  9330:   const trackGroup=Boolean(cutTrack&&ruleTrack&&cutTrack===ruleTrack);
  9331:   const unitExact=Boolean(cutUnit&&ruleUnit&&(cutUnit===ruleUnit||cutUnit.includes(ruleUnit)||ruleUnit.includes(cutUnit)));
  9332:   const unitGroup=Boolean(cutGroups.length&&ruleGroups.length&&cutGroups.some(x=>ruleGroups.includes(x)));
  9333:   // 모집단위가 양쪽에 명시됐는데 계열까지 충돌하면 같은 근거로 묶지 않습니다.
  9334:   if(cutUnit&&ruleUnit&&!unitExact&&cutGroups.length&&ruleGroups.length&&!unitGroup)return {ok:false,reason:'모집단위 불일치'};
  9335:   // 전형명이 양쪽에 명시된 경우 정확일치 또는 표준 전형군 일치가 필요합니다.
  9336:   if(cutName&&ruleName&&!trackExact&&!trackGroup)return {ok:false,reason:'전형군 불일치'};
  9337:   if(trackExact)return {ok:true,level:4,reason:'전형명 정확일치'};
  9338:   if(trackGroup&&unitExact)return {ok:true,level:4,reason:'전형군+모집단위 일치'};
  9339:   if(trackGroup&&unitGroup)return {ok:true,level:3,reason:'전형군+모집계열 일치'};
  9340:   if(trackGroup&&(!cutUnit||!ruleUnit))return {ok:true,level:2,reason:'전형군 일치·한쪽 모집단위 미기재'};
  9341:   // 한 DB에 전형명이 비어 있어도 모집단위가 명확히 연결되면 허용합니다.
  9342:   if((!cutName||!ruleName)&&(unitExact||unitGroup))return {ok:true,level:2,reason:unitExact?'모집단위 일치':'모집계열 일치'};
  9343:   return {ok:false,reason:'동일 전형 근거 부족'};
  9344: }
  9345: function strictAdmissionCutLimit(value){
  9346:   if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return null;
  9347:   const direct=Number(value);if(Number.isFinite(direct)&&direct>=1&&direct<=9)return direct;
  9348:   const values=(String(value).replace(/,/g,'').match(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?/g)||[]).map(Number).filter(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=1&&v<=9);
  9349:   if(!values.length)return null;
  9350:   // 복수 숫자가 들어간 문자열은 더 엄격한 값(작은 등급)으로 판정하여 오탐을 막습니다.
  9351:   return Math.min(...values);
  9352: }
  9353: function studentUniversityBadgeEvidence(student){
  9354:   const cacheKey=`${readonlyCache?.syncedAt||''}|${student.id||student.studentNo||''}|badge-evidence-v6-discovery`;

 10953: function programMessageDraft(program,mode){
 10954:   const masters=programStudentMasters(program);
 10955:   if(mode==='individual'){
 10956:     const base=programNoticeText(program);
 10957:     return masters.map(student=>`[${student.studentNo||''} ${student.name||'학생'}]\n${student.name||'학생'} 학생, 아래 프로그램 참여 내용을 확인하세요.\n${base}`).join('\n\n----------------\n\n');
 10958:   }
 10959:   if(mode==='sms'){
 10960:     const rows=masters.map(student=>`${student.studentNo||''}\t${student.name||''}\t${student.phone&&student.phone!=='-'?student.phone:'연락처 없음'}`);
 10961:     return `${programNoticeText(program)}\n\n[대상 학생]\n${rows.join('\n')}`;
 10962:   }
 10963:   if(mode==='riro') return `${programNoticeText(program)}\n\n※ 대상 학생은 반드시 프로그램 참여 여부와 보고서 작성 여부를 확인하기 바랍니다.`;
 10964:   if(mode==='family') return programFamilyLetterText(program);
 10965:   return programNoticeText(program);
 10966: }

 10990: function xmlEscape(value){return String(value??"").replace(/&/g,"&amp;").replace(/</g,"&lt;").replace(/>/g,"&gt;").replace(/\"/g,"&quot;");}
 10991: function safeFileName(value){return String(value||"출석부").replace(/[\\/:*?\"<>|]/g,"_").slice(0,80);}
 10992: function exportProgramAttendanceExcel(program){
 10993:   const rows=(program.students||[]).map((student,index)=>[index+1,student.studentNo||student.number||"",student.name||"",student.className?`${student.className}반`:"","",""]);
 10994:   const meta=[["프로그램명",program.actualTitle||program.title||""],["운영일",program.date||""],["운영시간",program.time||""],["장소",program.place||""],["교과",program.subject||""],["담당부서",program.department||""],["담당교사",program.teacher||""]];
 10995:   const rowXml=(cells)=>`<Row>${cells.map(cell=>`<Cell><Data ss:Type=\"String\">${xmlEscape(cell)}</Data></Cell>`).join("")}</Row>`;
 10996:   const xml=`<?xml version=\"1.0\"?><Workbook xmlns=\"urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:spreadsheet\" xmlns:ss=\"urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:spreadsheet\"><Worksheet ss:Name=\"출석부\"><Table>${meta.map(rowXml).join("")}<Row></Row>${rowXml(["번호","학번","이름","반","출석","비고"])}${rows.map(rowXml).join("")}</Table></Worksheet></Workbook>`;
 10997:   const blob=new Blob(["\ufeff",xml],{type:"application/vnd.ms-excel"}); const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob); const a=document.createElement("a"); a.href=url; a.download=`${safeFileName(program.actualTitle||program.title)}_출석부.xls`; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove(); setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000); toast("출석부 Excel 파일을 생성했습니다.");
 10998: }

 11000: function openProgramDrawer(id, options={}) {
 11001:   const localProgram = state.programs.find((p) => p.id === id);
 11002:   const connectedProgram = (readonlyCache?.programs || []).map(programWithOverride).find((p) => p.id === id);
 11003:   const program = localProgram || connectedProgram;
 11004:   if (!program) return;
 11005:   const students = (program.students || []).map((student)=>{
 11006:     const report=resolveStudentProgramReport(program,student);
 11007:     return {
 11008:       ...student,
 11009:       studentId:student.studentId||"",
 11010:       studentNo:student.studentNo||student.number||"",
 11011:       number:student.number||student.studentNo||"-",
 11012:       name:student.name||"-",
 11016:       reportSubmitted:Boolean(report),
 11017:       reportSubmittedAt:report?.submittedAt||student.reportSubmittedAt||"",
 11018:       reportId:report?.id||student.reportId||"",
 11019:       reportMatchStatus:report?.matchStatus||student.reportMatchStatus||""
 11020:     };
 11021:   }).sort((a,b)=>Number(programStudentGrade(a))-Number(programStudentGrade(b))||String(a.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.studentNo)));
 11022:   const isReadonly = Boolean(connectedProgram && !localProgram);
 11023:   const evidence = state.programEvidence?.[id] || [];
 11024:   const admin = state.programAdminReports?.[id] || {};
 11025:   const dueDate = program.reportToAuthority ? (program.reportDueDate || addDateDays(program.date, program.reportAfterDays || 0)) : "";

 11366: function openDormOutingDrawer() {
 11367:   const viewKey = dateKey(outingViewDate);
 11368:   const liveOutings = (readonlyCache?.dormOutings || []).filter(item=>!dormOutingExcludedByRule(item));
 11369:   const items = liveOutings.filter((item) => item.date === viewKey);
 11370:   const monday = new Date(outingViewDate);
 11371:   const day = monday.getDay();
 11372:   monday.setDate(monday.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
 11373:   const sunday = new Date(monday);
 11374:   sunday.setDate(sunday.getDate() + 6);
 11375:   const weekItems = liveOutings.filter((item) => item.date >= dateKey(monday) && item.date <= dateKey(sunday));
 11376:   const displayDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ko-KR", { year: "numeric", month: "long", day: "numeric", weekday: "short" }).format(outingViewDate);
 11377:   $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "DORMITORY · OUTING";
 11378:   $("#drawerTitle").textContent = `학사외출 · ${displayDate}`;
 11379:   const outingMasterStudent = (item) => {
 11380:     const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
 11381:     const ids=[item.studentId,item.studentNo,item.studentNumber].filter(Boolean).map(String);
 11382:     const byId=students.find(student=>ids.some(id=>id===String(student.id||"")||id===String(student.studentId||"")||id===String(student.studentNo||"")));
 11383:     if(byId)return byId;
 11384:     const name=String(item.name||"").trim();
 11385:     const cls=String(item.className||item.class||"").replace(/\D/g,"");
 11386:     const no=String(item.number||item.no||"").replace(/\D/g,"");
 11387:     return students.find(student=>String(student.name||"").trim()===name && (!cls||String(student.className||"").replace(/\D/g,"")===cls) && (!no||String(student.number||"").replace(/\D/g,"")===no)) || null;
 11388:   };
 11389:   const outingStudentNo = (item) => {
 11390:     const master=outingMasterStudent(item);
 11391:     const masterNo=String(master?.studentNo||"").replace(/\.0$/,"").trim();
 11392:     if (/^[123]\d{3}$/.test(masterNo)) return masterNo;
 11393:     const direct = String(item.studentNo || item.studentNumber || "").replace(/\.0$/,"").trim();
 11394:     if (/^[123]\d{3}$/.test(direct)) return direct;
 11395:     const classDigits = String(master?.className||item.className || item.class || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
 11396:     const numberDigits = String(master?.number||item.number || item.no || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
 11397:     if (!classDigits || !numberDigits) return "-";
 11398:     return `${classDigits}${numberDigits.padStart(2, "0")}`;
 11399:   };
 11400:   const outingEmail=(item)=>String(item.email||item.studentEmail||outingMasterStudent(item)?.email||"").trim();
 11401:   const rows = (list) => list.length ? list.map((item) => `<div class="outing-row"><time>${item.date.slice(5).replace("-", "/")}</time><span><b>${escapeHtml(outingStudentNo(item))} ${escapeHtml(item.name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.className)}반 ${escapeHtml(item.number)}번 · ${dormOutingIsMedical(item)?'<b class="state positive">병원</b> ':''}${escapeHtml(item.category || "외출")}</small></span><time>${escapeHtml(item.outTime || "-")}</time><time>${escapeHtml(item.returnTime || "-")}</time><span><b>${escapeHtml(item.reason || "-")}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.destination || "-")}</small></span><span class="state">${escapeHtml(item.status || "예정")}</span></div>`).join("") : `<div class="outing-empty">표시할 학사외출 기록이 없습니다.</div>`;
 11402:   const weekDays = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, index) => {
 11403:     const date = new Date(monday);
 11404:     date.setDate(date.getDate() + index);
 11405:     const key = dateKey(date);
 11406:     const list = weekItems.filter((item) => item.date === key);
 11407:     return `<section><header><b>${["월", "화", "수", "목", "금","토","일"][index]}</b><span>${key.slice(5).replace("-", "/")}</span><em>${list.length}명</em></header>${list.length ? list.map((item) => `<div class="outing-week-item"><time>${escapeHtml(item.outTime || "-")}–${escapeHtml(item.returnTime || "-")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.className)}반 ${escapeHtml(item.number)}번 · ${dormOutingIsMedical(item)?'<b class="state positive">병원</b> ':''}${escapeHtml(item.category || "외출")}</small><span>${escapeHtml(item.reason || "-")}</span></div>`).join("") : "<p>신청 없음</p>"}</section>`;

 12458: function uepSelectionDataset(){
 12459:   const rows=uepActiveSelectionRows(),dorm=uepDormStudentIds(),applications=[];
 12460:   rows.forEach(row=>{
 12461:     const student=row.__student,avg=uepStudentGradeAverage(student.id);
 12462:     ["2-1","2-2","3-1","3-2"].forEach(term=>uepSelectionTermSubjects(row,term).forEach(subject=>applications.push({term,subject,row,student,avg,dorm:dorm.has(String(student.id||student.studentNo||""))||dorm.has(String(student.studentNo||""))})));
 12463:   });
 12464:   const subjects=new Map();
 12465:   applications.forEach(app=>{const key=`${app.term}|${app.subject}`;if(!subjects.has(key))subjects.set(key,{key,term:app.term,subject:app.subject,students:[]});subjects.get(key).students.push(app);});
 12466:   subjects.forEach(group=>{group.students.sort((a,b)=>(a.avg??99)-(b.avg??99)||String(a.student.studentNo).localeCompare(String(b.student.studentNo)));const graded=group.students.filter(x=>x.avg!=null).length;group.students.forEach((x,i)=>{x.expectedRank=x.avg==null?null:i+1;x.expectedGrade=x.avg==null?"-":uepExpectedGrade(i+1,graded);});group.sectionCount=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(group.students.length/30));});
 12467:   const errors=rows.flatMap(row=>uepSelectionErrors08105(row).map(error=>({row,student:row.__student,...error})));
 12468:   return {rows,applications,subjects:[...subjects.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.term.localeCompare(b.term)||b.students.length-a.students.length||a.subject.localeCompare(b.subject,"ko")),errors};
 12469: }
 12470: function uepSelectionErrors08105(row){
 12471:   const errors=uepSelectionErrors(row),terms=["2-1","2-2","3-1","3-2"];
 12472:   const seen=new Map();
 12473:   terms.forEach(term=>{
 12474:     const subjects=uepSelectionTermSubjects(row,term);
 12475:     if(!subjects.length&&!errors.some(x=>x.type==="신청 누락"&&x.term===term))errors.push({type:"신청 누락",term,detail:"해당 학기 선택과목이 비어 있습니다."});
 12476:     subjects.forEach(subject=>{const normalized=subject.replace(/\s+/g,"");if(!seen.has(normalized))seen.set(normalized,[]);seen.get(normalized).push(term);});
 12477:   });
 12478:   seen.forEach((subjectTerms,subject)=>{if(subjectTerms.length>1)errors.push({type:"학기간 동일과목 중복",term:subjectTerms.join(" · "),detail:`${subject} 과목이 여러 학기에 중복 신청되었습니다.`});});
 12479:   return errors;
 12480: }

 12578: function uepSelectionErrors(row){
 12579:   const errors=[];
 12580:   const terms=["2-1","2-2"];
 12581:   const science=/(물리|화학|생명과학|지구과학|역학|에너지|전자기|양자|세포|물질대사|유전|지구시스템|융합과학)/;
 12582:   const social=/(세계사|동아시아|사회와 문화|사회문화|윤리|지리|정치|법과 사회|경제|인문학|사회문제)/;
 12583:   terms.forEach(term=>{const subjects=uepSelectionTermSubjects(row,term);if(subjects.some(x=>science.test(x))&&subjects.some(x=>social.test(x)))errors.push({type:"문·이과 교차지원",term,detail:subjects.join(" · ")});});
 12584:   const first=uepSelectionTermSubjects(row,"2-1"),second=uepSelectionTermSubjects(row,"2-2");
 12585:   const hierarchy=[
 12586:     [/(역학과 에너지|전자기와 양자)/,/물리학/ ,"물리"],
 12587:     [/(물질과 에너지|화학 반응의 세계)/,/화학/ ,"화학"],
 12588:     [/(세포와 물질대사|생물의 유전)/,/생명과학/ ,"생명과학"],
 12589:     [/지구시스템과학/,/지구과학/ ,"지구과학"]
 12590:   ];
 12591:   hierarchy.forEach(([advanced,basic,label])=>{if(second.some(x=>advanced.test(x))&&!first.some(x=>basic.test(x)))errors.push({type:"과학과목 위계오류",term:"2-1 → 2-2",detail:`${label} 선이수 과목 확인 필요`});});

 12593:   const l1=/중국/.test(lang1)?"중국어":/일본/.test(lang1)?"일본어":"";
 12594:   const l2=/중국/.test(lang2)?"중국어":/일본/.test(lang2)?"일본어":"";
 12595:   if((l1||l2)&&l1!==l2)errors.push({type:"제2외국어 연계오류",term:"2-1 → 2-2",detail:`${lang1||"미신청"} → ${lang2||"미신청"}`});
 12596:   const art1=String(row["2-1 예술"]||""),art2=String(row["2-2 예술"]||"");
 12597:   const a1=/음악/.test(art1)?"음악":/미술/.test(art1)?"미술":"";
 12598:   const a2=/음악/.test(art2)?"음악":/미술/.test(art2)?"미술":"";
 12599:   if((a1||a2)&&a1!==a2)errors.push({type:"예술과목 연계오류",term:"2-1 → 2-2",detail:`${art1||"미신청"} → ${art2||"미신청"}`});
 12600:   if(!first.length||!second.length)errors.push({type:"신청 누락",term:!first.length?"2-1":"2-2",detail:"해당 학기 선택과목이 비어 있습니다."});
 12601:   return errors;
 12602: }
 12603: 
 12604: // __UEP_08115_SDGS_SUPPLEMENT__
 12605: function uepSdgsSupplementPanel(){
 12606:   const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>String(s.id)===String(recordStudentId));
 12607:   if(!student)return "";
 12608:   const bundle=studentRecordBundle(student),reports=bundle.reports||[],activities=bundle.activities||[],programs=bundle.programs||[];
 12609:   const questions=[];
 12610:   if(!reports.length)questions.push("참여 활동에서 새롭게 알게 된 사실과 생각이 달라진 지점을 구체적으로 적어 주세요.");
 12611:   if(!activities.length)questions.push("교실·동아리·봉사활동에서 발견한 사회·공동체·환경 문제는 무엇인가요?");
 12612:   if(!programs.length)questions.push("관심 진로와 연결하여 다음에 더 탐구하고 싶은 문제와 실천 계획을 적어 주세요.");
 12613:   if(!questions.length)questions.push("기존 근거 중 가장 의미 있는 활동을 골라 문제 발견 → 탐구 → 변화 → 후속 실천의 순서로 보완해 주세요.");
 12614:   const text=`[SDGs 근거 보완 안내] ${student.studentNo||""} ${student.name||""}\n`+questions.map((x,i)=>`${i+1}. ${x}`).join("\n");
 12615:   return `<section class="sdgs-supplement-panel"><header><div><small>EVIDENCE SUPPLEMENT</small><h3>SDGs 근거 보완</h3><p>현재 연결된 활동 ${activities.length}건 · 프로그램 ${programs.length}건 · 보고서 ${reports.length}건을 기준으로 보완 질문을 제안합니다.</p></div><button class="btn primary" data-copy-sdgs-supplement>보완 질문 복사</button></header><ul>${questions.map(x=>`<li>${escapeHtml(x)}</li>`).join("")}</ul><textarea id="sdgsSupplementText" readonly>${escapeHtml(text)}</textarea></section>`;
 12616: }
 12617:

 12619: // __UEP_08116_SELECTION_SMS_HELPER__
 12620: function uepSelectionSms(student,errors){
 12621:   const lines=errors.map((x,i)=>`${i+1}. ${x.type}: ${x.detail}`).join("\n");
 12622:   return `[운호고 선택과목 보완 안내]\n${student.name} 학생의 2학년 선택과목 신청에서 확인이 필요한 내용이 있습니다.\n${lines}\n신청 내용을 확인한 뒤 담당 선생님의 안내에 따라 수정해 주세요.`;
 12623: }
